These findings identify the practical regime in which pretrained representations are most valuable and suggest that instance discrimination, rather than forest semantics, is the main remaining obstacle to a general-purpose 3D forest foundation model. Code and models are available at: https://github.com/prs-eth/ForPT.

</details>


### [206] [Streaming Video Editing with Easy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24788)
*Yujia Hu,Jiajun Li,Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SVEET框架，仅在预训练双向视频扩散模型上训练，即可支持自回归式高质量流式视频编辑，在单张H100上达到15 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到视频（video-to-video）扩散方法多依赖双向模型，难以直接适配流式（自回归）生成；将其转化为流式视频编辑存在特征纠缠与因果性冲突的问题亟待解决。

Method: 系统回顾已有video-to-video扩散方法，提炼出流式适配的两条关键原则：骨干特征解耦（backbone feature disentanglement）与条件帧独立（conditional frame independence）。据此提出新范式：辅助模型分支以时间独立自注意力编码源视频输入，并将中间特征注入对应骨干块以实现流式兼容控制；同时提出解耦训练方案，显式约束视频可控性与模型因果性优化方向的正交性，以弥合双向与流式模型特征空间差异。

Result: 大量实验表明SVEET在保持实时性能的同时取得更优编辑质量，在单张H100 GPU上无需任何辅助加速技术即达15 FPS。

Conclusion: 通过特征解耦与解耦训练，SVEET实现了双向扩散模型向流式编辑的零样本知识迁移，兼顾高质量与实时性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose SVEET, a framework that requires merely training on a pretrained bidirectional video diffusion model but supports high-quality streaming video editing in an auto-regressive fashion. To tackle this problem, we first systematically revisit existing video-to-video diffusion approaches and identify two key principles for such streaming adaptation: backbone feature disentanglement and conditional frame independence. Building on these insights, we develop a novel paradigm for controllable video generation. At its core, an auxiliary model branch encodes source video inputs with temporally independent self-attention, and the intermediate features are injected into the corresponding backbone blocks for streaming-compatible control. Moreover, to bridge the discrepancy between the feature spaces of bidirectional and streaming models, we propose a decoupled training scheme that explicitly enforces the orthogonality between the optimization directions of video controllability and model causality. Such disentanglement ensures compatibility between the two objectives at inference and facilitates smooth zero-shot knowledge transfer across heterogeneous backbone architectures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SVEET achieves superior editing quality while maintaining real-time performance, attaining 15 FPS on a single H100 GPU 17 without any auxiliary acceleration techniques. Codes are available at https://github.com/YujiaHu1109/SVEET.

</details>


### [207] [INTCORT: Training-Free Spatial Reasoning Enhancement for Vision-Language Models via Input Transformations and Confidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24813)
*Haoran Sun,Jingqi Xu,Yanhui Li,Enci Liu,Kaidi Xu,Yanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练空间推理增强框架 INTCORT，通过输入变换构建多推理视图并利用关系标记置信度路由聚合预测，在多个基准上平均提升 10.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在多模态任务上表现优异，但空间推理能力仍较弱。现有增强方法存在缺陷：依赖训练的方法计算开销大且易发生灾难性遗忘；免训练方法则干扰模型内部机制，损害通用能力。

Method: 作者首先验证两个假设：(1) 适当的几何图像变换与查询反转变换可纠正错误的空间预测；(2) 正确预测的关系标记（relation-token）置信度高于错误预测。基于此提出 INTCORT：一种免训练框架，通过输入变换构建多个推理视图，并依据关系标记置信度进行路由聚合，不修改 VLM 内部机制。

Result: 在多个常用基准上，INTCORT 显著提升多种 VLM 的空间推理准确率，所有模型与基准平均提升 10.01%；与既有方法相比，最高提升达 25.01%。

Conclusion: INTCORT 以免训练、不干扰模型内部机制的方式有效增强 VLM 空间推理能力，性能优于现有依赖训练和免训练方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in multimodal tasks, yet they still exhibit poor ability in spatial reasoning. Existing training-dependent and training-free enhancement methods suffer from high computational costs with catastrophic forgetting and internal mechanism interference that compromises general capabilities, respectively. In this work, we first verify two key hypotheses: appropriate geometric image transformation and query-reversal transformation can recover incorrect spatial predictions, and correct predictions exhibit higher relation-token confidence than incorrect ones. Based on these findings, we propose INTCORT, a training-free spatial reasoning enhancement framework that constructs multiple inference views through input transformations and aggregates their predictions via relation-token confidence routing, without modifying the VLM's internal mechanisms. Experimental results on several commonly-used benchmarks demonstrate that INTCORT substantially improves spatial reasoning accuracy across diverse VLMs, achieving an average improvement of 10.01% over all models and benchmarks. Compared with prior works, INTCORT achieves superior performance with improvements of up to 25.01%.

</details>


### [208] [Mobile Imaging Solutions for Medical Diagnosis: Trends and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24814)
*Syed Muhammad Ibne Zulfiker,Tanzima Hashem,Fariha Tabassum Islam,Md Sultanul Arifin,Khandker Aftarul Islam,Nishat Anjum Bristy,Faria Huq,Priyeta Saha,Syeda Nahida Akter,Arpita Saha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一篇综述系统梳理移动设备图像分析在医疗诊断中的研究现状，按应用类别对比现有方案，提炼理想工具特征，归纳挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理能力、摄像技术和移动图像分析的进步使智能手机、笔记本电脑等移动设备日益适用于医疗诊断与健康应用，且有望为偏远和资源受限地区的人群提供低成本、可及的医疗服务。

Method: 综述性研究：对移动图像驱动的医疗诊断现有研究进行梳理，按不同健康应用类别（如眼科与耳鼻喉疾病、营养不良、心率变异性、皮肤与口腔疾病、创伤等）对最先进方案进行对比分析，考察其优势与局限。

Result: 基于对比分析，提炼出移动图像诊断工具应具备的理想特征，并指出已有方法取得进展的领域以及仍需进一步研究的方向；同时讨论应用特定挑战与共性挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究全面概述了移动图像医疗解决方案及其在低成本疾病诊断与监测方面的潜力，尤其对偏远和资源受限环境中的弱势人群具有重要价值，并给出未来研究方向。

Abstract: Advances in processing power, camera technologies, and mobile image analysis have made smartphones and other mobile devices, such as laptops, increasingly suitable for medical diagnosis and healthcare applications. Researchers have developed low-cost solutions for the early detection and monitoring of various health conditions, including eye and ENT diseases, malnutrition, heart rate variability, skin and oral conditions, and injuries, using images captured by non-medical devices such as smartphones and webcams. This survey examines existing research on mobile image-based medical diagnosis, with an emphasis on its potential to enable low-cost and accessible healthcare. We comparatively analyze state-of-the-art solutions across different healthcare application categories, examining their advantages and limitations. Based on this analysis, we identify desirable characteristics of mobile image-based diagnostic tools and highlight areas where existing approaches have made progress as well as areas requiring further research. We also discuss application-specific and common challenges and outline directions for future research. Overall, this study provides a comprehensive overview of mobile image-based healthcare solutions and their potential to support low-cost disease diagnosis and monitoring, particularly for underserved populations in remote and resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [209] [ZVeC: A Zero-Shot Framework for Instance-Level Vehicle Extraction and Generative Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24825)
*Daisy Li,Kyle Gao,Quanyun Wu,Boris Jutzi,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZVeC 是一个零样本、实例驱动的点云补全框架，将场景级补全重构为物体级重建，在地下停车场 LiDAR 基准上取得优于场景级基线的效果，并在极端稀疏输入下保持几何一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下环境中的 LiDAR 点云因遮挡和视角受限而严重几何不完整，且缺乏大规模监督数据集，使可靠的场景级点云补全十分困难。

Method: 将场景分解为语义物体实例，对每个分割出的车辆实例，用深度与 3D 高斯条件化的扩散模型独立补全，利用泛化几何先验，再将补全后的实例重组成原始场景，全程无需场景特定训练。

Result: 在自建的真实地下停车场稠密 LiDAR 基准上，定量指标与视觉质量均稳定优于代表性场景级基线。补全点云与实测输入差异显著（平均 KL 散度约 2.1），但将输入降至原始 LiDAR 测量的 1% 时，补全结果仅有微小变化（KL 散度 < 0.50）。

Conclusion: ZVeC 通过物体级组合式重建，在无监督数据条件下实现了场景级点云补全，并在极端输入稀疏下仍能生成几何一致的补全结果。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds acquired in underground environments exhibit severe geometric incompleteness due to occlusions and limited sensor viewpoints, making reliable point cloud completion challenging without large supervised datasets. We propose ZVeC, a zero-shot, instance-driven framework that reformulates scene-level completion as compositional object-level reconstruction. By decomposing a scene into semantic object instances, ZVeC reduces reconstruction ambiguity in cluttered environments while eliminating the need for scenario-specific training. Each segmented vehicle is completed independently using a depth- and 3D Gaussian-conditioned diffusion model that exploits generalized geometric priors before the reconstructed instances are recomposed into the original scene. To evaluate our approach, we construct a real-world dense LiDAR benchmark of underground parking environments. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements over representative scene-level baselines in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. The completed point cloud differs substantially from the measured input (average KL divergence ~ 2.1), yet reducing the input to only 1% of the original LiDAR measurements changes the completed reconstruction only marginally (KL divergence < 0.50). This demonstrates that ZVeC produces geometrically consistent completions even under extreme input sparsity.

</details>


### [210] [When Wider Views Fail: Stress-Testing Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24839)
*Daisy Li,Kyle Gao,Quanyun Wu,Hanna Chomko,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究前馈3D重建模型在视角变化这一分布偏移下的失效模式，通过固定输入数量但改变视角跨度，发现视角跨度增大导致重建性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型能从稀疏图像高效估计几何，但其预训练特性使其在训练数据分布之外易受影响。识别这些失效模式对于判断模型能否在无约束成像场景中可靠部署至关重要。

Method: 将视角变化作为受控的分布偏移，在保持输入图像预算固定的前提下改变稀疏输入图像的视角跨度，并在多个前馈重建模型上进行测试。

Result: 随着视角跨度增大，多个前馈重建模型出现显著性能退化；大视角跨度同时导致表面覆盖不完整和缺乏观测图像支撑的几何。

Conclusion: 视角变化会引发超出传统重建不完整性之外的失效模式，表明需要在挑战模型所学几何先验的分布偏移条件下评估预训练前馈模型。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models enable efficient geometry estimation from sparse images, but their pretrained nature can make them vulnerable to distribution shifts beyond their training data. Identifying these failure modes is important for understanding when such models can be reliably deployed in unconstrained imaging settings. We investigate viewpoint variation as a controlled distribution shift by varying the angular span of sparse image inputs while keeping the input budget fixed. Across multiple feed-forward reconstruction models, we observe substantial degradation as viewpoint span increases, with wide spans producing both incomplete surface coverage and geometry unsupported by the observed imagery. These results reveal that viewpoint variation can induce failure modes beyond conventional reconstruction incompleteness, highlighting the need to evaluate pretrained feed-forward models under distribution shifts that challenge their learned geometric priors.

</details>


### [211] [Revisiting Multi-View Stereo: A Sequence-to-Sequence Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24850)
*Aoxiang Fan,Corentin Dumery,Nicolas Talabot,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将已知相机参数的多视图立体（MVS）问题重构为序列到序列任务，提出融合相机先验的全局Transformer架构，在多公开基准上超越传统MVS与前馈方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期前馈（FF）模型可联合估计3D几何与相机参数，但即使提供真值相机参数，仍因重建歧义导致几何畸变。作者希望在已知相机参数的MVS设定下缓解该问题，并弥合传统MVS与前馈方法之间的差距。

Method: 不再将MVS视为仅对单一参考视图预测深度的sequence-to-one映射，而是类比FF模型重构为对所有输入视图联合预测几何的sequence-to-sequence任务。提出全局Transformer架构，含两个显式利用相机先验的组件：（1）ray-map嵌入，将相机参数注入图像patch token，使Transformer具备相机感知能力；（2）统一的全局cost volume，替代传统逐视图cost volume，联合捕获所有视图的3D结构。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，该方法达到state-of-the-art性能，超越传统MVS和前馈重建基线。

Conclusion: 通过将已知相机参数的MVS重构为序列到序列的全视图联合几何预测，并显式引入相机诱导先验（ray-map嵌入与全局cost volume），可有效缓解重建歧义带来的几何畸变，统一了传统MVS与前馈方法的优势。

Abstract: Computing accurate geometry from multi-view images is a fundamental problem in computer vision. Recent feed-forward (FF) models jointly estimate 3D geometry and camera parameters, but they typically suffer from geometry distortion caused by reconstruction ambiguity, even when ground-truth camera parameters are supplied. In this paper, we study the multi-view stereo (MVS) problem with known camera parameters and propose a novel approach that bridges conventional MVS and FF methods. Rather than casting MVS as a sequence-to-one mapping that predicts depth only for a single reference view, we reformulate it as a sequence-to-sequence task, akin to FF models, that jointly predicts geometry for all input views. We introduce a global transformer-based architecture with two components that explicitly exploit camera-induced priors: ray-map embeddings that inject camera parameters into image patch tokens, making the transformer camera-aware, and a unified global cost volume that replaces conventional per-view cost volumes to jointly capture 3D structure across all views. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks show our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing both MVS and FF reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [212] [DTKDP: A Dual Teacher Knowledge Distillation and Pruning Framework for Lightweight Oriented SAR Ship Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24872)
*Yuming Li,Fan Zhang,Alin M. Achim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双教师知识蒸馏与剪枝（DTKDP）框架，用于轻量化旋转框SAR舰船检测，在参数量与FLOPs大幅下降的同时保持接近全尺寸模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段旋转框检测器精度高，但其庞大骨干网络、特征金字塔、候选区域模块与沉重的RoI头难以部署；现有轻量SAR舰船检测器多为单阶段框架，缺乏候选级精修，旋转定位精度不足。

Method: 在卷积、归一化与线性层中引入可学习门控，以剪枝卷积通道与RoI头神经元；提出旋转候选对齐（RPA），在教师生成的共享旋转候选空间中蒸馏师生预测；采用双教师方案，由同构主教师提供分类与回归指导，异构辅教师提供补充分类线索。

Result: 在SSDD与RSDD-SAR上，DTKDP将ResNet-50骨干的Oriented R-CNN与RoI Transformer参数量减少87.5-91.8%，FLOPs减少75.6-79.9%；所得Oriented R-CNN-slim与RoI Transformer-slim在AP、mAP上接近全尺寸模型，AP50、AP75、mAP50:75、mAP50:95相对变化为下降2.38%至提升0.65%；相比RTMDet-tiny，四项指标在两个数据集上提升0.52-27.55%，并持续优于代表性蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: DTKDP在旋转框SAR舰船检测中实现了良好的精度-效率权衡，验证了剪枝与双教师蒸馏结合用于轻量化两阶段检测器的有效性。

Abstract: Two-stage oriented detectors achieve high localization accuracy in synthetic aperture radar (SAR) ship detection, but their large backbones, feature pyramids, proposal modules, and heavy region of interest (RoI) heads hinder deployment. Existing lightweight SAR ship detectors typically use one-stage frameworks that lack proposal-level refinement for precise rotated localization. This paper presents a dual-teacher knowledge distillation and pruning (DTKDP) framework for lightweight oriented SAR ship detection. DTKDP introduces learnable gates into convolutional, normalization, and linear layers to prune convolutional channels and RoI-head neurons. Rotated proposal alignment (RPA) distills teacher and student predictions in a shared teacher-generated rotated proposal space, while a dual-teacher scheme combines classification and regression guidance from a homogeneous main teacher with complementary classification cues from a heterogeneous auxiliary teacher. Experiments on the SAR Ship Detection Dataset (SSDD) and Rotated Ship Detection Dataset in SAR Images (RSDD-SAR) show that DTKDP reduces the parameters of Oriented Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Oriented R-CNN) and RoI Transformer equipped with ResNet-50 backbones by 87.5-91.8% and their floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 75.6-79.9%. In terms of average precision (AP) and mean average precision (mAP), the resulting Oriented R-CNN-slim and RoI Transformer-slim retain accuracy close to their full-scale counterparts. Relative changes across $\mathrm{AP}_{50}$, $\mathrm{AP}_{75}$, $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:75}$, and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ range from a 2.38% decrease to a 0.65% improvement. Compared with RTMDet-tiny, they improve all four metrics on both datasets by 0.52-27.55% and consistently surpass representative distillation methods, demonstrating a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [213] [SPHQuant: Efficient extreme low bit weight quantization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24875)
*Kewei Zhang,Zheng Chen,Haotong Qin,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPHQuant 是一种面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的无旋转变权的 spherical 权重量化框架，通过将 8D 权重向量分解为坐标符号、半径和正向单位方向，把离群值幅度集中到半径并额外分配精度，从而在 2-3 比特极端低比特下保持精度且无需旋转开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生多模态 VLM 正成为下一代基础模型的核心形态，但其大语言主干参数量大、自回归解码受内存带宽约束，导致边缘部署困难。权重量化后训练（PTQ）是可行方案，但推向极低比特（2-3 bit）仍难：无旋转方法在低比特下受离群值影响严重，基于旋转的方法虽提升精度却引入额外运行开销。

Method: 提出 SPHQuant，一种无旋转的 spherical 权重量化框架。不再直接在笛卡尔坐标量化权重，而是将每个 8D 权重向量分解为坐标符号、半径和正向单位方向：该表示将离群值幅度隔离到半径，使方向保持有界且统计规律。基于此，对半径分配额外精度以缓解离群值带来的精度下降；使用紧凑的正向方向码本，并通过角度参数化微调码本条目以保持单位球约束。此外设计硬件友好的 GEMV 内核，使方向码本足够小以放入共享内存查找，并高效打包径向比特。

Result: 实验显示 SPHQuant 在精度上匹配当前最先进的极端低比特量化方法，同时在 RTX A6000 上解码吞吐相较 QTIP 提升 30.3%。代码将发布于 https://github.com/Pushazf/SPHQuant。

Conclusion: SPHQuant 通过 spherical 分解与半径精度分配，在不引入旋转运行开销的前提下实现了 VLM 极端低比特量化，兼顾精度与解码效率，为边缘部署提供实用方案。

Abstract: Recent foundation models are moving toward native multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs), making VLMs a central form of next-generation foundation models. However, their large language backbones make edge deployment difficult due to high memory footprint and memory-bound autoregressive decoding. Weight-only post-training quantization is a practical solution, but pushing VLMs to extreme low bit-widths remains challenging: existing rotation-free methods suffer from outliers at 2-3 bits, while rotation-based methods improve accuracy at the cost of additional runtime overhead. We propose SPHQuant, a rotation-free spherical weight-only quantization framework for VLMs. Instead of quantizing weights directly in Cartesian coordinates, SPHQuant decomposes each 8D weight vector into coordinate signs, radius, and a positive unit direction. This representation isolates outlier magnitude into the radius while keeping directions bounded and statistically regular. Based on this insight, SPHQuant allocates extra precision to the radius to mitigate accuracy degradation induced by outliers. It further uses a compact positive-direction codebook and fine-tunes codebook entries through angular parameterization to preserve the unit-sphere constraint. We also design a hardware-friendly GEMV kernel that keeps the direction codebook small enough for shared-memory lookup and packs radial bits efficiently. Experiments show that SPHQuant matches the performance of state-of-the-art extreme low-bit quantization methods while improving decode throughput over QTIP by 30.3% on RTX A6000. Code will be released in https://github.com/Pushazf/SPHQuant.

</details>


### [214] [Generating Chest X-Ray Counterfactuals by Specialising Foundation Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24879)
*Xiaodan Xing,Rajat R. Rasal,Julia A. Meister,Sara Ghorayeb,Galvin Khara,Jessica Schrouff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 specialisation 框架，将预训练非因果生成模型适配为分布偏移下的因果机制，并据此构建放射学反事实图像生成模型 RadCF，在三个胸片数据集上验证其数据与参数高效性及反事实合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中反事实图像生成的应用受限于标注数据稀缺、数据集间分布偏移，以及预训练生成模型与反事实推断所需模型之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出 specialisation——一种数据与参数高效的框架，用于在分布偏移下将预训练非因果生成模型适配为因果机制；基于该框架，使用 latent flow matching 训练放射学反事实图像生成模型 RadCF。

Result: 在三个涵盖不同数据集偏移、数据量与反事实问题、且涉及高局部化干预的胸片数据集上验证：RadCF 与 specialisation 在数据与参数高效的同时，相较现有方法提升了反事实合理性，且生成的反事实能够检测并缓解下游医学分类器中的捷径学习。

Conclusion: specialisation 框架与 RadCF 为分布偏移下的医疗反事实图像生成提供了数据与参数高效的解决方案，并可服务于下游医学分类器的鲁棒性诊断与改进。

Abstract: Counterfactual image generation answers questions about how a subject would have looked under retrospective, hypothetical scenarios. Recent methods have improved perceptual quality, identity preservation and faithfulness to an underlying causal model, but their adoption in healthcare is limited by scarce annotated data, distribution shift between datasets, and mismatches between pretrained generative models and those required for counterfactual inference. We propose specialisation, a data and parameter-efficient framework for adapting pretrained, non-causal generative models into causal mechanisms under distribution shift. Based on this framework, we train a radiology counterfactual image generation model, called RadCF, using latent flow matching. We validate our approach on three chest X-ray datasets spanning different dataset shifts, data volumes, and counterfactual questions, associated with challenging, highly-localised interventions. Our results show that RadCF and specialisation improve counterfactual soundness over existing methods while being data and parameter efficient, and that the resulting counterfactuals can detect and mitigate shortcut learning in a downstream medical classifier. Code is available at https://github.com/GSK-AI/RadCF/.

</details>


### [215] [SLICEChat: Progressive In-Encoder Token Pruning for Whole-Slide Pathology Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24894)
*Ali Kerem Bozkurt,Baris Cem Bakay,Ibrahim Kulac,Cigdem Gunduz-Demir,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLICEChat 是一种面向全切片病理图像的切片级多模态大模型，将渐进式 token 剪枝嵌入混合 Mamba–Transformer 编码器，在多模态融合前压缩切片表征，实现高精度且高效的吉像素级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片病理图像（WSI）包含吉像素级视觉内容，现有方法需处理数千个 patch token 且仅在切片编码后才压缩，导致多模态注意力计算代价高昂，可扩展性差。

Method: 提出 SLICEChat：在混合 Mamba–Transformer 切片编码器中进行渐进式 token 剪枝；Mamba 层实现高效长程传播，Transformer 层在序列逐步缩短时保留全局交互；阶段之间采用语言监督、区域感知的剪枝策略，在受控保留率调度下移除空间连贯的低效用区域，从而在多模态融合前得到紧凑切片表征。

Result: 在 SlideBench VQA 上，TCGA 队列准确率 79.84%，BCNB 队列 59.09%，优于既有切片级病理 MLLM；在 WSI-Bench 上取得最高整体指标，同时具备有竞争力的显存占用和评估模型中的较低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法可在吉像素级 WSI 上实现准确且计算高效的多模态推理。

Abstract: Whole-slide pathology images (WSIs) contain gigapixel-scale visual content, creating a major scalability challenge for slide-level multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing approaches process thousands of patch tokens and typically apply compression only after slide encoding, leaving multimodal attention computationally expensive. We introduce SLICEChat, a slide-level MLLM that integrates progressive token pruning within a hybrid Mamba--Transformer slide encoder. Mamba layers enable efficient long-range propagation, while Transformer layers preserve global interactions as the sequence is progressively shortened. Between stages, language-supervised, region-aware pruning removes spatially coherent low-utility regions under a controlled keep-rate schedule, producing compact slide representations before multimodal fusion. On SlideBench VQA, SLICEChat achieves 79.84% accuracy on TCGA and 59.09% on BCNB cohorts, outperforming prior slide-level pathology MLLMs, and achieves the highest overall WSI-Bench metrics. It also provides competitive memory usage and the inference latency among the evaluated models. These results demonstrate accurate and computationally efficient multimodal reasoning over gigapixel WSIs.

</details>


### [216] [PixelDiT2: Representation-Grounded Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24919)
*Yongsheng Yu,Wei Xiong,Yichen Sheng,Shiqiu Liu,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelDiT2提出"表征锚定"策略，用冻结的预训练视觉基础模型为像素空间扩散模型的每个patch提供显式表征引导，从而解耦表征学习与像素生成，在ImageNet-256上600轮达到FID 1.46，512×512上680轮达到FID 1.48。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型虽已缩小与潜空间扩散的图像质量差距，但仍收敛更慢、最终质量落后。作者认为关键原因是缺乏显式表征先验：潜空间扩散在紧凑结构化隐空间中去噪，而像素扩散需同时从原始RGB空间学习适合去噪的表征并进行像素生成，负担过重。

Method: 提出PixelDiT2，一种端到端像素空间扩散模型，在不引入自编码器、不产生潜空间重建瓶颈的前提下解耦表征学习与像素生成。核心机制为"表征锚定"（representation grounding）：使用冻结的预训练视觉基础模型，在整个去噪过程中为每个patch提供显式的逐块表征引导，使像素扩散Transformer能更专注于像素生成。

Result: 在ImageNet-256×256上，训练600个epoch后FID为1.46；在512×512分辨率上，训练680个epoch后FID为1.48。

Conclusion: 显式表征先验是弥补像素空间扩散相对潜空间扩散在收敛速度与最终质量上差距的有效途径；通过冻结视觉基础模型提供逐patch引导，可在无自编码器与重建瓶颈的端到端框架下取得具有竞争力的生成质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in pixel-space diffusion models have narrowed the image quality gap with latent-space diffusion, but still converge more slowly and lag behind in final image quality. We argue that a key reason is the lack of an explicit representation prior: unlike latent diffusion, which usually denoises in a compact and structured latent space, pixel diffusion needs to learn denoising-friendly representations and pixel generation simultaneously from raw RGB space. To address this problem, we propose PixelDiT2, an end-to-end pixel-space diffusion model designed to decouple representation learning from pixel generation without introducing an autoencoder or latent reconstruction bottleneck. We propose representation grounding that uses a frozen pretrained vision foundation model to provide explicit per-patch representation guidance throughout denoising, allowing the pixel diffusion transformer to focus more on pixel generation. On ImageNet-256x256, PixelDiT2 achieves an FID of 1.46 after 600 epochs; at 512x512 resolution, PixelDiT2 achieves an FID of 1.48 after 680 epochs.

</details>


### [217] [Anatomy-Decomposed Chest Computed Tomography (CT) Projections as Scalable Supervision for Bone Suppression in Chest Radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24937)
*Mrunmay Angaitkar,Piyush Kumar,Aarjav Satia,Pranav Rao,Ashish Mittal,Manoj Tadepalli,Preetham Putha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于CT数字重建放射图(DRR)的配对监督框架，实现胸片骨骼/肺成分抑制，无需真实配对数据即可迁移到真实X光片，并在多数据集上验证其检测增益与图像真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片中骨骼重叠会遮盖异常，而骨骼抑制任务缺乏配对的训练数据，限制了监督学习的应用。

Method: 构建DRR框架，将胸部CT转为配对监督：先用新的骨骼分割算法将CT分解为骨骼、非肺软组织与肺三部分，分别投影后加权组合，得到像素配准且各成分之和恰等于整体DRR的合成放射图。在其上训练模型预测目标成分并通过相减恢复剩余部分，从而迁移到真实胸片。进一步以模型在真实胸片上的输出作为目标域，进行无配对的成分级DRR翻译，缩小外观差异同时保留解剖细节。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的下游检测实验表明骨骼抑制有效，增益集中于骨骼重叠严重的异常。与应用于同一CT的开源DRR引擎相比，未修改的DRR在真实感和标签相关解剖结构保留上相当，经翻译的DRR在所评估方法中取得最佳FID、肺野清晰度及与源CT解剖的一致性。

Conclusion: 该DRR配对监督框架可在无真实配对数据条件下实现骨骼与肺成分抑制并迁移至真实胸片，成分级翻译进一步提升图像真实性，代码与投影数据已公开。

Abstract: Bone overlap can obscure abnormalities in chest radiographs, while scarce paired training data limit supervised bone suppression. We address this challenge with a digitally reconstructed radiograph (DRR) framework that converts chest computed tomography (CT) into paired supervision for component suppression. A novel bone segmentation algorithm enables CT decomposition into bone, non-lung soft-tissue, and lung components, which are projected separately. Their weighted combination yields synthetic radiographs with pixel-registered component images that sum exactly to the full DRR. Models trained on these data suppress bone or lung components by predicting the target component and recovering the remainder by subtraction, transferring to real radiographs without real paired training data. As an extension, their outputs on real radiographs provide target domains for unpaired, component-wise DRR translation, reducing the appearance gap while retaining anatomical details. Across multiple public datasets, downstream detection experiments demonstrate the utility of bone suppression, with gains concentrated on abnormalities with substantial bone overlap. Compared with open-source DRR engines applied to the same CTs, our unmodified DRRs achieve comparable realism and preservation of label-relevant anatomy, while translated DRRs achieve the best Fréchet inception distance (FID), lung-field sharpness, and agreement with source-CT anatomy among the evaluated methods. Models and inference code: https://huggingface.co/qureaiorg/bone-suppression; Translated projections: https://huggingface.co/datasets/qureaiorg/ct2xr-projections.

</details>


### [218] [GAE: Learning a Geometry-Native Latent Space for 3D-Consistent World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24981)
*Jiahao Lu,Minghao Yin,Wenbo Hu,Hengyu Liu,Wang Zhao,Sai-Kit Yeung,Ying Shan,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出几何原生自编码器（GAE），将几何基础模型特征重参数化为紧凑隐空间，使隐变量可联合解码为外观、深度、相机和点图，从而作为感知与生成的共享接口，显著提升生成图像的视觉质量与3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成器能生成照片级真实帧，却难以保持一致的3D场景；作者认为这不仅是建模问题，更是表示问题——生成器通常演化以外观为中心的隐变量，而感知模型在语义丰富且编码跨视图结构的空间中恢复几何。因此需要一种几何原生的共享隐表示。

Method: 提出几何原生自编码器（GAE），不将几何作为额外输出，而是把几何基础模型的特征重参数化为紧凑隐空间；该隐空间可联合解码为外观、深度、相机与点图。基于此隐状态，使用标准条件流（conditional flow）支持多种生成任务。

Result: 在固定生成器与训练协议的受控对比中，用GAE替换原隐变量后，视觉质量与独立测量的3D一致性均提升：在RealEstate10K和DL3DV上FVD分别下降12.7%和23.1%，RealEstate10K上相机轨迹误差减半。

Conclusion: 结果表明隐空间是几何一致生成的核心，可作为感知与生成之间的共享接口。

Abstract: We present a compact geometry-native latent space as a shared foundation for perception and generation. Visual generators can produce photorealistic frames without preserving a consistent 3D scene. We argue that this is not only a modeling problem but also a representation problem: generators typically evolve appearance-centric latents, while perception models recover geometry in a semantically rich space that encodes cross-view structure. Rather than adding geometry as another output, we reparameterize a geometry foundation model's features into a compact latent space for generation. We realize this shift with the geometry-native autoencoder (GAE), whose latent is jointly decodable to appearance, depth, cameras, and point maps. With this state, a standard conditional flow supports diverse generation tasks. In controlled comparisons that hold the generator and training protocol fixed, replacing the latent with GAE improves both visual quality and independently measured 3D coherence: FVD falls by $12.7\%$ and $23.1\%$ on RealEstate10K and DL3DV, and camera-trajectory error is halved on RealEstate10K. Together, these results show that the latent space is central to geometry-consistent generation and can serve as a shared interface between perception and generation.

</details>


### [219] [WorldCrafter: Consistent Video World Model with Implicit 3D-aware Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24984)
*Wangbo Yu,Kunhao Liu,Wenbo Hu,Shenghai Yuan,Chaoran Feng,Haiyang Zhou,Yukun Huang,Yiran Wang,Wang Zhao,Yingmin Luo,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldCrafter 提出了一种可相机查询的隐式三维感知记忆，使视频世界模型在长时域与跨视角场景中保持先验观测一致性，实现分钟级流式场景探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型支持动态环境的交互式探索，但难以在长时域和跨视角下持续遵循先前的观测结果。

Method: 让目标视角决定多视角证据如何压缩进视频生成器有限的 token 预算；训练一个与视频生成器联合优化的记忆编码器与位姿条件读取模块，在去噪前将历史观测整合为固定的目标视角特定 token，无需显式基于深度的对应关系；结合近期时序上下文与少步蒸馏。

Result: 在静态与动态场景实验中，长时域一致性与相机控制精度显著提升，同时在分钟级探索中保持视觉质量。

Conclusion: WorldCrafter 通过隐式三维感知记忆实现从单张图像或文本提示出发的流式场景探索，验证了相机查询式记忆对长时域一致性与相机可控性的有效性。

Abstract: Video world models enable interactive exploration of dynamic environments, yet struggle to respect prior observations over long horizons and across viewpoints. We present WorldCrafter, a video world model that learns a camera-queryable implicit 3D-aware memory for this purpose. The key insight is to let the requested viewpoint shape how multi-view evidence is compressed into the video generator's limited token budget. Trained jointly with the video generator, a memory encoder and pose-conditioned readout module integrate historical observations into a fixed set of target view-specific tokens before denoising, without explicit depth-based correspondences. By combining this memory with recent temporal context and few-step distillation, WorldCrafter enables streaming scene exploration from a single input image or text prompt. Experiments across static and dynamic scenes show substantial gains in long-horizon consistency and camera-control accuracy while preserving visual quality during minute-scale exploration.

</details>


### [220] [VideoGen-Agent: Reinforcing Video Generation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24997)
*Binxu Li,Haoyi Duan,Yuhui Zhang,Yaohui Zhang,Zihao Lin,Kaituo Feng,Suozhi Huang,Xiangyi Li,Yu Li,Chunyuan Li,Shilong Liu,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VideoGen-Agent，一个通过多任务智能体强化学习训练的多模态智能体，能调用外部工具完成视频生成，并发布 VABench 基准。在 VABench 上相较基础文生视频模型提升 19.1 分（56.5→75.6），升级生成工具后进一步达到 86.1，人工评估中 84.3% 的比较偏好该配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能生成高保真、时序连贯的视频，但难以满足需要专业知识、特定身份、物理一致性或有序事件的提示。作者希望让模型学会使用外部工具来弥补这些能力短板，实现跨视频生成任务的工具使用学习。

Method: 训练多模态智能体 VideoGen-Agent：(1) 通过多轮交互协调增强、生成与验证三类工具，依据提示与中间观测进行决策；(2) 在覆盖六类任务的类别均衡数据集上训练共享策略；(3) 先用教师生成轨迹做监督微调以建立工具使用行为，再用强化学习精调；(4) 采用类别感知混合奖励，评估工具调用有效性、任务适配的工具选择及生成视频质量。同时构建 600 条提示的留出基准 VABench，涵盖流程性知识、单/多实体身份保持、物理一致性、场景构图与多镜头时序结构。

Result: 在 VABench 上，VideoGen-Agent 相比其基础文生视频生成器提升 19.1 分（56.5 提升至 75.6）；仅升级生成工具（无需额外智能体训练）可进一步将得分提升至 86.1；人工评估中，84.3% 的比较更偏好升级配置而非最强独立基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持在多种视频生成任务中学习工具使用，并表明训练好的智能体能够受益于生成工具的后续进步，即工具升级可带来即时的性能增益。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have enabled high-fidelity, temporally coherent video generation. However, these models often struggle to satisfy prompts requiring specialized knowledge, specific identities, physical consistency, or ordered events. In this paper, we present VideoGen-Agent, a multimodal agent trained through multitask agentic reinforcement learning to use external tools for video generation. The agent coordinates augmentation, generation, and verification tools through multi-turn interactions, using the prompt and intermediate observations to guide its decisions. We train a shared policy on a category-balanced dataset spanning six tasks. Supervised fine-tuning on teacher-generated trajectories establishes tool-use behavior, which is then refined through reinforcement learning. A category-aware hybrid reward evaluates tool-call validity, task-appropriate tool use, and generated video quality. We further introduce VABench, a held-out benchmark of 600 prompts covering procedural knowledge, single- and multi-entity identity preservation, physical consistency, scene composition, and multi-shot temporal structure. On VABench, VideoGen-Agent improves over its base text-to-video generator by 19.1 points, from 56.5 to 75.6. Upgrading the generation tools further raises the score to 86.1 without additional agent training. Human raters prefer the upgraded configuration over the strongest standalone baseline in 84.3% of comparisons. These results support learning tool use across video-generation tasks and show that the trained agent can benefit from subsequent advances in generation tools.

</details>


### [221] [GameHorizon Suite: Multi-Horizon Data and Evaluation in Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25001)
*Yiran Wang,Xingyilang Yin,Junfu Pu,Guangzhi Wang,Kaifeng Li,Mingyu Ouyang,Huiqiang Sun,Lingen Li,Cheng Cheng,Wangbo Yu,Honghao Chen,Xiaodong Cun,Chi-Man Pun,Zhiguo Cao,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GameHorizon统一数据与评测套件，跨多时间跨度衡量游戏能力，含标注流水线、大规模AAA数据集与离线/在线基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子游戏可作为AI模型的测试平台，综合考察视觉理解、指令分解、目标规划与精确动作控制；但现有数据集与基准覆盖游戏范围窄、缺少语言指令、依赖高方差在线 rollout，难以标准化比较。

Method: GameHorizon Suite包含三部分：(1) GameHorizon-Annotator，可扩展的自动化多跨度指令标注流水线；(2) GameHorizon-Data，基于该流水线构建的首个大规模AAA游戏数据集，含时间对齐视频、玩家动作与多跨度指令，来自21款游戏、100名人类专家玩家的5000小时录制；(3) GameHorizon-Bench，提供可复现的离线评测与逐步在线测试，离线含数千标准化问题（3类主任务及系列诊断变体），在线检验离线分数是否反映真实游戏能力并将长跨度失败定位到具体步骤。

Result: 基于该套件评测47个模型，超过一百万次模型调用，揭示清晰的任务难度层级与模型能力间的显著差异。

Conclusion: GameHorizon为跨时间跨度与模型家族的游戏能力评测提供标准化标尺，并将公开数据集、标注器与基准以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Modern video games provide a measurable testbed for AI models, combining abilities of visual understanding, instruction decomposition, goal planning, and precise action control over multiple temporal horizons. Existing datasets and benchmarks, however, either cover a narrow range of games, lack language instructions, or rely on high-variance online rollouts. To address these challenges, we introduce GameHorizon, a unified data and evaluation suite that measures gameplay capabilities at different horizons for diverse model families. GameHorizon Suite consists of three components. First, GameHorizon-Annotator is a scalable and automated annotation pipeline for multi-horizon instructions. Second, utilizing the pipeline, we construct GameHorizon-Data, the first large-scale AAA gameplay dataset with temporally aligned videos, player actions, and multi-horizon instructions. It comprises 5,000 hours of recordings from 21 games, collected by 100 human expert players. Third, we build GameHorizon-Bench with reproducible offline and stepwise online testing. The offline track enables reproducible evaluation using thousands of standardized questions organized into three primary tasks and a series of diagnostic variants, while the online track tests whether offline scores reflect actual gameplay capabilities and localizes failures to specific steps within long-horizon gameplay. Based on our GameHorizon Suite, we evaluate 47 models through more than one million model invocations, revealing a meaningful hierarchy of task difficulty and pronounced differences in model capabilities. Our work can provide a standardized yardstick for evaluating gameplay capabilities across horizons and model families. We will release our dataset, annotator, and benchmark to facilitate future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Causal Localization of the Refusal Direction in Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22260)
*Leonardo Haw-Yang Foo,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文通过因果干预（causal intervention）方法，在五个跨三个骨干家族的大型音频语言模型（LALM）上，探究语音拒绝行为的来源，发现拒绝依赖主要定位于语言模型中后层，而非音频前端接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LALM通过在已安全对齐的文本语言模型前接入语音前端构建而成。当模型拒绝有害语音请求时，尚不清楚这种拒绝是由音频前端承载，还是继承自文本语言模型。厘清这一来源对模型安全审计至关重要。

Method: 在音频到语言模型的接口以及测试的语言模型残差层处，拟合区分有害与良性提示的方向向量，消融该方向分量，并测量模型首token拒绝边际（refusal margin）的变化。五个模型中有四个在留出类别偏移（held-out category shift）下评估。同时通过置零编码器输出验证音频通路是否仍在使用。

Result: 在五个跨三骨干家族的LALM中（三个通过基线安全门），最大效应出现在语言模型中后层，而接口方向消融影响很小。在Qwen2.5-Omni上，消融L16方向使边际变化-7.10，而投影器处仅-0.013；置零编码器输出使边际变化-4.7，说明音频通路仍在使用。该对比在早期层即可线性解码，且单层消融影响甚微；仅用语言模型骨干拟合的方向可迁移至完整音频模型。

Conclusion: 这些干预定位的是拒绝边际的依赖来源，而非拒绝的计算位置。移动边际并不总是改变模型输出内容。对这些模型的安全审计应采用干预而非仅依赖探针（probe），并应同时审视继承的文本语言模型与音频接口。

Abstract: A large audio language model (LALM) attaches a speech front end to a text language model (LM) that is already safety-aligned. When such a model refuses a harmful spoken request, is the refusal carried by the front end, or inherited from the text LM? We test this with causal interventions. At each model's audio-to-LM interface and at tested LM residual layers, we fit a direction separating harmful from benign prompts, ablate its component, and measure the resulting change in the model's first-token refusal margin. Four of the five models are evaluated under held-out category shift. Across five LALMs spanning three backbone families, with three models passing a baseline safety gate, the largest tested effects occur in a mid-to-late LM band, while ablations of the tested interface directions have little effect. On Qwen2.5-Omni, ablating the L16 direction changes the margin by -7.10, versus -0.013 at the projector. The audio pathway is still in use: zeroing the encoder output changes the margin by -4.7. In the same model, the contrast is linearly decodable at an early layer where single-layer ablation has little effect, and a direction fitted on the LM backbone alone transfers to the full audio model. Because harmful and benign prompts also differ in form, we interpret the direction as refusal-linked rather than harmfulness-specific. These interventions localize dependence of the refusal margin, not where refusal is computed. Moving the margin also does not always change what the model writes. Safety audits of these models should use interventions rather than rely on probes alone, and should examine the inherited text LM alongside the audio interface.

</details>


### [223] [Hi-Singers: A Comprehensive High-Quality Dataset for Expressive Audio-Driven Singing Head Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22264)
*Yichi Zhang,Hui Zhang,Guanjun Liu,Yuefeng Zou,Fengzhao Sun,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Hi-Singers，首个面向歌唱头部合成的大规模高质量自�然场景视频数据集，包含29,608个片段、约170小时，并建立跨语言与音乐风格的评测基准，验证其能显著提升多类模型的视觉真实感、唇音同步与节奏动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动数字人生成在说话头合成已实现照片级效果，但扩展到歌唱头合成面临显著域差距：歌唱需要更夸张表情、明显张口与精确节奏同步。现有模型多基于语音数据集训练，容易出现"节奏漂移"与动态受限问题，且缺乏专门的歌唱数据与评测基准。

Method: 构建Hi-Singers数据集：经严格自动化与人工筛选流程，保证主题一致、高分辨率与运动稳定；共29,608个视频片段、约170小时；并设计跨语言和音乐风格均衡的专用评测基准；在3D系数类与扩散类等多种架构上进行广泛实验。

Result: Hi-Singers在所有维度上一致且显著提升模型性能：视觉真实感更强、唇音同步一致性更高、节奏动态更精确，有效弥合域差距，为歌唱合成任务设定新的性能标准。

Conclusion: Hi-Singers作为首个大规模、高质量、自然场景的歌唱头合成数据集，配合专用基准，证明数据层面的域适配可显著缓解节奏漂移与动态受限问题，推动歌唱头合成研究，数据集已公开。

Abstract: State-of-the-art models for audio-driven digital human generation have achieved photo-realistic results in talking-head synthesis. However, extending these models to singing-head synthesis remains challenging due to a significant Domain Gap: singing requires more exaggerated expressions, vivid jaw openings, and precise rhythmic synchronization. Current models, primarily trained on speech datasets, often struggle with "rhythmic drift" and constrained dynamics. To address this, we introduce Hi-Singers, the first large-scale, high-quality, in-the-wild video dataset specifically tailored for singing head synthesis. Hi-Singers undergoes a rigorous automated and manual filtering pipeline, ensuring strict thematic adherence, high-resolution rendering, and stable motion, resulting in 29,608 video segments totaling approximately 170 hours. We further establish a dedicated evaluation benchmark balanced across linguistic and musical styles. Extensive experiments across diverse architectures, including 3D-coefficient and diffusion-based models, demonstrate that Hi-Singers consistently and significantly improves performance across all dimensions. Specifically, it enables models to achieve superior visual realism, enhanced lip-sync consistency, and more precise rhythmic dynamics, effectively bridging the domain gap and setting a new performance standard for the singing synthesis task. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/CharlesZhang-USTC/Hi-Singers

</details>


### [224] [Visual Graph Reasoning via Knowledge Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22327)
*Rongzheng Wang,Zhe Wang,Ke Qin,Rongwei Wang,Muquan Li,Yizhuo Ma,Yihong Huang,Jielei Wang,Shuang Liang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出VGCompiler，以知识编译为核心的视觉图推理范式，通过表示编译器恢复保结构的中间图表示、操作编译器将查询意图编译为可执行图操作，并以分层奖励强化学习训练，8B模型超越闭源VLM基线28.9%、代码基线23.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉图推理需直接从图图像作答，拓扑与状态以视觉而非符号形式呈现。现有VLM方法连简单视觉图问题都失败，根本原因在于其只重视最终答案监督，忽视了从视觉输入中恢复显式、保拓扑与状态的图表示这一中间环节。

Method: 提出VGCompiler编译中心范式：表示编译器从视觉输入恢复保结构的中间图表示，操作编译器在恢复的图状态上把查询意图编译为可执行图操作。基于Qwen3-VL-8B，采用强化学习训练，奖励分层覆盖可执行性、编译图有效性、表示质量与操作质量；并用冻结观察器将图与问题条件摘要为轻量签名，支持档案检索与代码复用。

Result: 在GVLQA、VisionGraph、VGCURE三个基准上，8B骨干的Qwen-VGCompiler较最强闭源VLM基线提升28.9%，较最强代码基线提升23.7%，同时保持高效率。在城轨路径规划、物流配送、网络故障评估三个真实领域亦验证其跨异构视觉图与领域任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将显式图表示恢复与可执行操作编译作为推理核心环节，配合分层奖励与签名检索复用，可显著提升视觉图推理的准确性与泛化性，为VLM处理结构化视觉任务提供新范式。

Abstract: Visual graph reasoning requires answering graph-theoretic questions directly from graph images, where graph topology and state are conveyed visually rather than given in symbolic form. Despite recent progress of vision-language models (VLMs), current approaches to visual graph reasoning still fail on simple visual graph problems. This reveals a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: they prioritize final-answer supervision over the intermediate recovery of an explicit graph representation that preserves graph topology and state from visual input. To address this limitation, we propose VGCompiler, a compilation-centric paradigm for visual graph reasoning via knowledge compilation. VGCompiler organizes reasoning around two compilers: a representation compiler that recovers a structure-preserving intermediate graph representation from visual input, and an operation compiler that compiles query intent under the recovered graph state into an executable graph operation. Specifically, we build VGCompiler on Qwen3-VL-8B and train it with reinforcement learning guided by a layered reward over executability, compiled graph validity, representation quality, and operation quality. VGCompiler uses a frozen observer to summarize graph and question conditions into lightweight signatures, enabling archive retrieval and code reuse across similar regimes. Experiments on three benchmarks GVLQA, VisionGraph, and VGCURE, show that Qwen-VGCompiler, built on an 8B backbone, surpasses the strongest closed-source VLM baseline by 28.9% and the strongest code-based baseline by 23.7%, while maintaining high efficiency. We further evaluate VGCompiler on three real-world domains, including metro routing, logistics delivery, and network fault assessment, where it generalizes across heterogeneous visual graphs and domain-grounded tasks.

</details>


### [225] [If You Hear It, Help Find It: Orthogonal Knowledge Distillation for Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Event Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23376)
*Yi Xu,Cheng Chen,Wenzhuo Lei*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该论文提出OV-OrthKD，一个可靠性感知的非对称蒸馏框架，用于开放词汇音视频事件定位（OV-AVEL），通过将不同可靠性教师信号合理"放置"来实现监督分配，在OV-AVEBench上取得0.816 segment AP并显著提升F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OV-AVEL任务中不同监督来源（视觉教师与音频教师）在时间边界可靠性上存在差异：在OV-AVEBench上，配置的视觉教师比音频教师提供更可靠的边界线索，尽管音频教师仍是强预训练模型且具语义信息。作者将这一挑战形式化为"监督放置"问题——哪些教师信号可以塑造定位决策，哪些应保持辅助角色。

Method: 提出OV-OrthKD，一个可靠性感知的非对称蒸馏框架。其包含：视觉特征迁移塑造决策对齐表示；音频特征迁移丰富互补的辅助子空间；文本原型锚定已见/未见类别语义；正交损失限制两个教师特定投影之间的方向重叠。推理时学生通过查询感知融合继续使用两种模态，而默认训练配方将音频教师监督保持在segment-logit路径之外。

Result: 在OV-AVEBench上，OV-OrthKD达到0.816 segment AP，相比官方微调基线，F1@0.5整体提升2.7个点，未见类别提升3.4个点。路径分配、角色交换、损坏与迁移分析一致支持监督放置作为OV-AVEL的任务特定设计轴。

Conclusion: 监督放置是开放词汇音视频事件定位中一个任务特定的核心设计轴；通过非对称蒸馏将视觉教师信号置于决策路径、音频教师置于辅助路径，可在保持多模态推理能力的同时提升定位性能。该设置特定的诊断不应被理解为视觉与音频的普遍优劣排序。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary audio-visual event localization (OV-AVEL) grounds a text-queried event in time from video, audio, and language. The supervision sources available to this task can differ in temporal-boundary reliability: on OV-AVEBench, our configured visual teacher gives more reliable boundary cues than the configured audio teacher, although the latter is a strong pretrained audio model and remains semantically informative. This is a setting-specific diagnostic rather than a universal ranking of vision and audio. We formulate the resulting challenge as supervision placement: which teacher signals may shape the localization decision, and which should remain auxiliary. Based on this view, we propose OV-OrthKD, a reliability-aware asymmetric distillation framework. Visual feature transfer shapes a decision-aligned representation, audio feature transfer enriches a complementary auxiliary subspace, a text prototype anchors seen/unseen category semantics, and an orthogonality loss limits directional overlap between the two teacher-specific projections. The student continues to use both modalities through query-aware fusion at inference, while the default training recipe keeps audio-teacher supervision off the segment-logit path. On OV-AVEBench, OV-OrthKD achieves 0.816 segment AP and improves F1@0.5 over the official fine-tuning baseline by 2.7 points overall and 3.4 points on unseen categories. Path-assignment, role-swap, corruption, and transfer analyses consistently support supervision placement as a task-specific design axis for OV-AVEL.

</details>
